<script data-order-margin="core">
/* 历史订单利润与仓储毛利统一口径。订单样本原收入与已核费用均保留。 */
(function (root) {
  'use strict';
  const packet = JSON.parse(document.getElementById('orderMarginEvidence').textContent);
  const finite = value => typeof value === 'number' && Number.isFinite(value);
  const costFields = ['head','tariff','handling','tail','platformFee','advertising','consumptionTax','installation'];
  const estimates = new Map(packet.rows.map(row => [row.key,row]));
  const rejected = new Map(packet.excluded.map(row => [row.key,row]));
  const missing = (reason = '缺订单出库仓或批次链') => ({
    scope:'missing',scopeLabel:reason,complete:false,storage:{lower:null,representative:null,upper:null}
  });

  function prepare(row) {
    if (row.withoutModelReserve || row.eligibleForMargin !== true) return row;
    const exclusion = rejected.get(row.key);
    if (exclusion) return {...row,eligibleForMargin:false,missingFields:[exclusion.reason],exclusion};
    const components = {...row.componentTotals};
    const reserve = components.aftersalesReserve;
    delete components.aftersalesReserve;
    const unitComponents = {...row.components};
    delete unitComponents.aftersalesReserve;
    if (![row.truePurchase,row.operatingPurchase,...costFields.map(key => components[key])].every(finite)) {
      return {...row,eligibleForMargin:false,missingFields:['实际或可核模型费用缺项']};
    }
    const common = costFields.reduce((sum,key) => sum+components[key],0);
    const op = row.revenue-common-row.operatingPurchase*row.quantity;
    const real = row.revenue-common-row.truePurchase*row.quantity;
    return {...row,withoutModelReserve:true,modelAftersalesReserve:finite(reserve)?reserve:null,componentTotals:components,components:unitComponents,
      pureOperatingProfitTotal:op,pureTrueProfitTotal:real,operatingMargin:op/row.revenue,trueMargin:real/row.revenue,
      operatingProfit:op/row.quantity,trueProfit:real/row.quantity};
  }

  function storageFor(sample) {
    const record = estimates.get(sample.key);
    if (!record || record.sku !== sample.sku || record.orderId !== sample.orderId || record.quantity !== sample.quantity) {
      return missing();
    }
    const fee = record.complete && finite(record.amount) ? record.amount / sample.quantity : null;
    return {...record,complete:fee !== null,storage:{lower:fee,representative:fee,upper:fee}};
  }

  function decorate(sample) {
    const row = prepare(sample), storage = storageFor(row);
    const fee = storage.complete ? storage.amount : null;
    const opGross = fee === null ? null : row.pureOperatingProfitTotal-fee;
    const trueGross = fee === null ? null : row.pureTrueProfitTotal-fee;
    return {...row,storageScenario:storage,storageFee:storage.storage.representative,
      storageFeeLower:storage.storage.lower,storageFeeUpper:storage.storage.upper,warehouseFeeTotal:fee,
      storageOperatingProfitTotal:opGross,storageTrueProfitTotal:trueGross,
      storageOperatingMargin:opGross === null ? null : opGross/row.revenue,
      storageTrueMargin:trueGross === null ? null : trueGross/row.revenue};
  }

  function warehouseScope(rows) {
    const known = rows.filter(row=>finite(row.warehouseFeeTotal));
    const calculate = subset => {
      const revenue = subset.reduce((total,row)=>total+row.revenue,0);
      const fee = subset.reduce((total,row)=>total+row.warehouseFeeTotal,0);
      const operatingGross = subset.reduce((total,row)=>total+row.storageOperatingProfitTotal,0);
      const trueGross = subset.reduce((total,row)=>total+row.storageTrueProfitTotal,0);
      return {revenue,fee,operatingGross,trueGross,
        operatingMargin:operatingGross/revenue,trueMargin:trueGross/revenue};
    };
    const subset = known.length ? calculate(known) : null;
    return {storageSampleCount:known.length,storageComplete:known.length===rows.length,
      storageActualCount:known.filter(row=>row.storageScenario.scope==='invoice').length,
      storageAllocatedCount:known.filter(row=>row.storageScenario.scope==='allocated_invoice').length,
      storageEstimateCount:known.filter(row=>!['invoice','allocated_invoice'].includes(row.storageScenario.scope)).length,
      storageSubset:subset,storageOperatingProfit:known.length===rows.length ? subset?.operatingGross ?? null : null,
      storageTrueProfit:known.length===rows.length ? subset?.trueGross ?? null : null,
      storageTotal:known.length===rows.length ? subset?.fee ?? null : null};
  }

  root.orderMarginCore = {prepare,decorate,storageFor,warehouseScope,packet};
})(window);
</script>
<script data-order-margin="model">
/* 利润证据独立于销量窗口：只计算同一SKU的有效订单，不向其他SKU借样本。 */
(function (root) {
  'use strict';
  const warehouseMargin = root.orderMarginCore;
  const data = JSON.parse(document.getElementById('profitPriceData').textContent);
  const finite = value => typeof value === 'number' && Number.isFinite(value);
  const num = value => finite(value) ? value : null;
  const samplesBySku = new Map(), cache = new Map();
  const records = new Map((data.orders.rows || []).map(row => [row.sku, row]));
  const platformName = value => value === '独立站' ? 'Shopify' : String(value || '');
  const storeName = (platform, value) => typeof sqStoreName === 'function' ? sqStoreName(platform, value).toLowerCase() : String(value || '').toLowerCase();
  const values = value => Array.isArray(value) ? value : value ? [value] : [];
  const channelMatches = (row, filters) => (!values(filters.platform).length || values(filters.platform).map(platformName).includes(platformName(row.platform)))
    && (!values(filters.store).length || values(filters.store).some(store => storeName(row.platform, store) === storeName(row.platform, row.store)));
  for (const raw of [...(data.orders.samples || []), ...(data.orders.rejectedSamples || [])]) {
    const row = warehouseMargin.prepare(raw);
    if (!samplesBySku.has(row.sku)) samplesBySku.set(row.sku, []);
    samplesBySku.get(row.sku).push(row);
  }
  // 与源数据声明的取样顺序一致，拒绝样本可能没有日期，不能让其阻断整个页面。
  for (const rows of samplesBySku.values()) rows.sort((a, b) => {
    for (const key of ['sampleDate', 'platform', 'store', 'orderId', 'key']) {
      const left = String(a[key] || ''), right = String(b[key] || '');
      if (left !== right) return left < right ? 1 : -1;
    }
    return 0;
  });
  const storageFor = warehouseMargin.storageFor;
  const withStorage = warehouseMargin.decorate;
  function aggregate(rows) {
    if (!rows.length) return null;
    const sum = key => rows.reduce((value, row) => value + row[key], 0);
    const revenue = sum('revenue'), dates = rows.map(row => row.sampleDate).sort();
    const platforms = [...new Set(rows.map(row => row.platform))], stores = [...new Set(rows.map(row => row.platform + ' / ' + row.store))];
    const storageKnown = rows.filter(row => finite(row.storageOperatingMargin) && finite(row.storageTrueMargin));
    const complete = storageKnown.length === rows.length;
    const storageRevenue = storageKnown.reduce((value, row) => value + row.revenue, 0);
    return { sampleCount: rows.length, sampleKeys: rows.map(row => row.key), periodStart: dates[0], periodEnd: dates.at(-1), revenue, quantity: sum('quantity'),
      platforms, stores, platformCount: platforms.length, storeCount: stores.length, singleChannelOnly: stores.length === 1,
      operatingProfit: sum('pureOperatingProfitTotal'), trueProfit: sum('pureTrueProfitTotal'),
      operatingMargin: sum('pureOperatingProfitTotal') / revenue, trueMargin: sum('pureTrueProfitTotal') / revenue,
      orderMeanOperatingMargin: sum('operatingMargin') / rows.length, orderMeanTrueMargin: sum('trueMargin') / rows.length,
      ...warehouseMargin.warehouseScope(rows),
      storageOperatingMargin: complete ? storageKnown.reduce((value, row) => value + row.storageOperatingMargin * row.revenue, 0) / storageRevenue : null,
      storageTrueMargin: complete ? storageKnown.reduce((value, row) => value + row.storageTrueMargin * row.revenue, 0) / storageRevenue : null,
      storageQuantity: complete ? sum('quantity') : null, basisLabel: '最近' + rows.length + '单综合 · 按收入加权' };
  }
  function scenariosFor(sku, filters) {
    return (data.prices.forecast_scenarios || []).filter(row => row.sku === sku && channelMatches(row, filters));
  }
  function reprice(raw, scenario) {
    const sample = warehouseMargin.prepare(raw);
    if (sample.eligibleForMargin !== true) return null;
    const total = scenario.after_total ?? scenario.total;
    if (!finite(total) || total <= 0 || sample.platform === 'Wayfair' || !finite(sample.revenue) || sample.revenue <= 0) return null;
    // 税/安装收入的拆分未齐时不把零售通知价当成该订单全部收入。
    if ((sample.componentTotals.consumptionTax || 0) !== 0 || (sample.componentTotals.installation || 0) !== 0) return null;
    const revenue = total * sample.quantity, ratio = revenue / sample.revenue;
    const components = { ...sample.componentTotals };
    const oldVariable = (components.platformFee || 0) + (components.advertising || 0);
    components.platformFee = (components.platformFee || 0) * ratio;
    components.advertising = (components.advertising || 0) * ratio;
    const change = revenue - sample.revenue - (components.platformFee + components.advertising - oldVariable);
    const op = sample.pureOperatingProfitTotal + change, real = sample.pureTrueProfitTotal + change;
    return { ...sample, referenceRevenue: sample.revenue, referencePrice: sample.price, referenceOperatingMargin: sample.operatingMargin, referenceTrueMargin: sample.trueMargin,
      revenue, price: total, componentTotals: components, pureOperatingProfitTotal: op, pureTrueProfitTotal: real, operatingProfit: op / sample.quantity,
      trueProfit: real / sample.quantity, operatingMargin: op / revenue, trueMargin: real / revenue, priceScenario: scenario,
      forecastMethod: '通知含运价替换收入；平台费和广告沿同单费率；采购、头程、关税、作业及尾程保持同单金额；模型售后预留单列' };
  }
  function forSku(sku, filters = {}) {
    const key = JSON.stringify([sku, filters.platform || '', filters.store || '', filters.evidenceMode || 'current']);
    if (cache.has(key)) return cache.get(key);
    const all = (samplesBySku.get(sku) || []).filter(row => channelMatches(row, filters));
    const valid = all.filter(row => row.eligibleForMargin === true && finite(row.pureOperatingProfitTotal) && finite(row.pureTrueProfitTotal) && row.revenue > 0 && row.quantity > 0);
    const samples = valid.slice(0, 20).map(withStorage), historical = aggregate(samples);
    const scenarios = scenariosFor(sku, filters).sort((a, b) => String(b.notice_at || b.date || '').localeCompare(String(a.notice_at || a.date || '')));
    const forecastRows = [];
    if (filters.evidenceMode !== 'withHistory') for (const row of valid) {
      const candidates = scenarios.filter(item => item.platform === row.platform && storeName(row.platform, item.store) === storeName(row.platform, row.store)
        && (!item.shop_sku || item.shop_sku.toUpperCase() === String(row.shopSku || row.sku).toUpperCase()));
      const listing = row.listingId || row.asin || row.listing_id;
      const matching = listing ? candidates.filter(item => item.listing_id === listing) : candidates;
      // 无Listing的历史订单，只有同店同SKU唯一通知价时才允许作带披露的预估。
      const uniquePrices = new Set(matching.map(item => item.after_total));
      const scenario = matching.length && uniquePrices.size === 1 ? matching[0] : null;
      if (!scenario) continue;
      const projected = reprice(row, scenario); if (projected) forecastRows.push(withStorage(projected));
      if (forecastRows.length === 20) break;
    }
    const forecast = aggregate(forecastRows), selected = forecast || historical;
    const rejected = all.filter(row => row.eligibleForMargin !== true);
    const result = { sku, historical, forecast, selected, samples: forecast ? forecastRows : samples, historicalSamples: samples,
      validSampleCount: valid.length, rejectedSampleCount: rejected.length, rejected, record: records.get(sku) || null,
      status: forecast ? '通知价预估' : historical ? '同SKU最近有效订单' : '无同SKU样本',
      isForecast: Boolean(forecast), isEstimate: true, events: (data.prices.events || []).filter(row => row.sku === sku),
      priceSummary: data.prices.by_sku?.[sku] || null, scenarios, noCrossSkuFallback: true };
    cache.set(key, result); return result;
  }
  root.ppProfitModel = { ready: true, version: data.version, data, forSku, aggregate, reprice, storageFor, channelMatches, clearCaches: () => cache.clear(),
    skuCount: records.size, sourceCutoff: data.orders.orderCutoff, storageDate: warehouseMargin.packet.snapshotDate, prepareSample: warehouseMargin.prepare, decorateSample: withStorage };
})(window);

</script>
<script>
/* 调价摘要与原证据：通知、生效回执和报价观察分别展示，不跨店拼累计。 */
(function (root) {
  'use strict';
  const finite = value => typeof value === 'number' && Number.isFinite(value);
  const esc = value => typeof cxEsc === 'function' ? cxEsc(value == null ? '' : value) : String(value == null ? '' : value).replace(/[&<>"']/g, ch => ({ '&': '&amp;', '<': '&lt;', '>': '&gt;', '"': '&quot;', "'": '&#39;' })[ch]);
  const pct = value => finite(value) ? (value * 100).toFixed(2) + '%' : '未列';
  const money = value => finite(value) ? '$' + value.toLocaleString('en-US', { minimumFractionDigits: 2, maximumFractionDigits: 2 }) : '未列';
  const small = value => '<small class="pp-scope">' + esc(value) + '</small>';
  const table = (headers, rows) => typeof cxTable === 'function' ? cxTable(headers, rows) : '<div class="cx-table-wrap"><table><thead><tr>' + headers.map(head => '<th>' + esc(head) + '</th>').join('') + '</tr></thead><tbody>' + rows.map(row => '<tr>' + row.map(cell => '<td>' + cell + '</td>').join('') + '</tr>').join('') + '</tbody></table></div>';
  const data = () => root.ppProfitModel?.data?.prices || { events: [], by_sku: {}, forecast_scenarios: [] };
  const platformName = value => value === '独立站' ? 'Shopify' : value;
  const list = value => Array.isArray(value) ? value : value ? [value] : [];
  const date = value => String(value || '日期未列').replace('T', ' ').replace(/:00\+08:00$/, '（北京时间）');
  const scopeLabel = scope => [scope.platform === 'Shopify' ? '独立站' : scope.platform, scope.store, scope.shop_sku].filter(Boolean).join(' / ') || '未逐店指定';
  const regionLabel = sku => /-WKC$/i.test(sku || '') ? '美西库存链接' : /-EKC$/i.test(sku || '') ? '美东库存链接' : '';

  function channelMatches(row, filters = {}) {
    if (root.ppProfitModel?.channelMatches) return root.ppProfitModel.channelMatches(row, filters);
    return (!list(filters.platform).length || list(filters.platform).map(platformName).includes(platformName(row.platform))) && (!list(filters.store).length || list(filters.store).includes(row.store));
  }
  function eventMatches(event, filters = {}) {
    if (event.exact_listing) return channelMatches(event, filters);
    const platforms = event.platform_scope || (event.platform ? [event.platform] : []);
    if (list(filters.platform).length && platforms.length && !list(filters.platform).map(platformName).some(name => platforms.includes(name))) return false;
    if (event.store && list(filters.store).length && !channelMatches({ platform: event.platform, store: event.store }, { store: filters.store })) return false;
    return true;
  }
  const eventsFor = (sku, filters) => (data().events || []).filter(event => event.sku === sku && eventMatches(event, filters));
  const chainsFor = (sku, filters) => (data().by_sku?.[sku]?.cumulative_by_scope || []).filter(chain => channelMatches(chain.scope, filters));
  const range = values => {
    const numbers = values.filter(finite);
    if (!numbers.length) return null;
    const low = Math.min(...numbers), high = Math.max(...numbers);
    return Math.abs(high - low) < .00005 ? pct(low) : pct(low) + '～' + pct(high);
  };

  function badges(sku, filters = {}) {
    const events = eventsFor(sku, filters).filter(event => event.badge_eligible);
    const down = events.some(event => event.direction === 'down'), up = events.some(event => event.direction === 'up');
    if (!down && !up) return '';
    return '<span class="pp-badges">' + (down ? '<span class="pp-badge" title="有历史降价通知或同链接报价下降；点开查看具体平台、店铺和证据状态">降价</span>' : '') +
      (up ? '<span class="pp-badge raise" title="有涨价/恢复价格通知或同链接报价上升；不与降价幅度相加">价格上升</span>' : '') + '</span>';
  }

  function summaryLine(sku, filters = {}) {
    const events = eventsFor(sku, filters).filter(event => event.badge_eligible);
    if (!events.length) return '';
    const chains = chainsFor(sku, filters), lines = [];
    for (const basis of [...new Set(chains.map(chain => chain.price_basis))]) {
      const group = chains.filter(chain => chain.price_basis === basis);
      const start = [...new Set(group.map(chain => chain.baseline_date))].sort(), end = [...new Set(group.map(chain => chain.latest_date))].sort();
      const down = range(group.filter(chain => chain.net_reduction_rate > .0001).map(chain => chain.net_reduction_rate));
      const up = range(group.filter(chain => finite(chain.upward_chain_rate) && chain.upward_chain_rate > .0001).map(chain => chain.upward_chain_rate));
      const netUp = range(group.filter(chain => chain.net_reduction_rate < -.0001).map(chain => -chain.net_reduction_rate));
      const period = (start.length > 1 ? start[0] + '～' + start.at(-1) : start[0]) + '→' + end.at(-1);
      const values = [down && '累计净降' + down, up && '观察回升' + up, netUp && '累计净涨' + netUp].filter(Boolean);
      if (!values.length) values.push('可比区间报价未变');
      lines.push(values.join('；') + ' · ' + basis + ' · 基准' + period + ' · ' + group.length + '链接');
    }
    const knownRaise = events.filter(event => event.record_type === 'notice' && event.direction === 'up' && (finite(event.increase_rate) || finite(event.increase_amount_usd)));
    for (const event of knownRaise) lines.push('历史' + date(event.date) + '回升通知：' + (finite(event.increase_rate) ? '+' + pct(event.increase_rate) : '+' + money(event.increase_amount_usd)) + '（' + (event.store || '店铺未列') + '）');
    if (!lines.length) lines.push('有调价通知；累计需同店同链接可比基价，详见调价历史');
    return '<span class="pp-change-line" title="累计仅覆盖标注基准至报价日期，非全部历史；不同店铺链接分别计算">' + lines.slice(0, 3).map(esc).join('<br>') + '</span>';
  }

  function sources(event) {
    const refs = event.source || [];
    if (!refs.length) return '<span class="pp-empty">原证据未列</span>';
    return '<details><summary>来源 ' + refs.length + ' 项</summary><div class="pp-source">' + refs.map(ref => {
      if (!ref) return '';
      const path = ref.path || ref.source_path || '', pointer = [ref.sheet, ref.row && '第' + ref.row + '行', ref.source_pointer, ref.original_event_line && '原观察第' + ref.original_event_line + '行', ref.snapshot_index != null && '原生快照' + ref.snapshot_index].filter(Boolean).join(' · ');
      return '<p>' + esc(path.split('/').pop() || '历史原文') + '<br>' + small(pointer || path) + (ref.source_excerpt ? '<br>' + small(ref.source_excerpt) : '') + '</p>';
    }).join('') + '</div></details>';
  }

  function priceCell(event) {
    if (finite(event.before_total) || finite(event.after_total)) return esc(money(event.before_total) + ' → ' + money(event.after_total)) + '<br>' + small('含运总价') +
      (finite(event.after_item) ? '<br>' + small('调后商品' + money(event.after_item) + ' + 运费' + money(event.after_shipping)) : '');
    if (finite(event.before_item) || finite(event.after_item)) return esc(money(event.before_item) + ' → ' + money(event.after_item)) + '<br>' + small(event.price_basis || '商品价');
    if (finite(event.before_price) || finite(event.after_price)) return esc(money(event.before_price) + ' → ' + money(event.after_price)) + '<br>' + small(event.price_basis || '原文价格口径');
    return '<span class="pp-empty">原文未列绝对价格</span>';
  }
  function changeCell(event) {
    if (event.direction === 'up') return esc(finite(event.increase_rate) ? '+' + pct(event.increase_rate) : finite(event.rate) ? '+' + pct(event.rate) : finite(event.increase_amount_usd) ? '+' + money(event.increase_amount_usd) : '涨幅未列');
    if (event.direction === 'down') return esc(finite(event.rate) ? '-' + pct(event.rate) : '原文仅列目标价') + '<br>' + small(event.rate_basis || '');
    return esc(event.event_kind === 'profit_reprice_plan' ? '利润重算方案，非统一售价降幅' : '更正/继续/方案，不新增降价轮次');
  }

  function detail(sku, filters = {}) {
    const events = eventsFor(sku, filters).sort((a, b) => String(b.notice_at || b.date || '').localeCompare(String(a.notice_at || a.date || '')) || a.event_id.localeCompare(b.event_id));
    const chains = chainsFor(sku, filters);
    if (!events.length && !chains.length) return '<section class="pp-section pp-events"><h3>调价历史</h3><p>当前来源未检出此SKU的明确调价记录；没有记录不代表从未调价。</p></section>';
    const scenarios = (data().forecast_scenarios || []).filter(row => row.sku === sku && channelMatches(row, filters));
    let html = '<section class="pp-section pp-events"><h3>调价历史与通知价预估</h3><p>通知发布时间、实际生效时间和报价快照分开展示。订单保留原成交价；通知价只用于标注范围内的预估场景。'+
      '11/17现场回看群内最新通知为11/16，未把尚未群发的4款草案记作已执行。</p>';
    if (scenarios.length) html += '<h4>通知价预估场景</h4><div class="pp-note pp-warning">下列参考店铺来自可比报价与通知价格的算术匹配；群通知未逐店指名，未核准确生效时间。不得套用至其他店铺或Wayfair供货价。</div>' + table(['参考平台 / 店铺 / Listing', '原报价 → 通知含运价', '通知时间 / 状态'], scenarios.map(row => [esc(scopeLabel(row)) + '<br>' + small(row.listing_id + ' ' + regionLabel(row.shop_sku)), esc(money(row.before_total) + ' → ' + money(row.after_total)) + '<br>' + small('调后商品' + money(row.after_item) + ' + 运费' + money(row.after_shipping)), esc(date(row.notice_at)) + '<br>' + small(row.snapshot_matches_notification ? '快照与通知价一致；准确生效时间未核' : '通知价预估；后台生效未核') ]));
    if (chains.length) html += '<h4>同店同链接可比区间</h4><p>净降幅＝1－最新可比价÷基准价。下调链和回升链分别连乘；价格恢复后，本期未变不代表历史从未降价或涨价。</p>' + table(['精确平台 / 店铺 / Listing', '基准 → 最新 / 口径', '累计净变化', '观察下调 / 回升'], chains.map(chain => [esc(scopeLabel(chain.scope)) + '<br>' + small(chain.scope.listing_id + ' ' + regionLabel(chain.scope.shop_sku)), esc(money(chain.baseline_price) + ' → ' + money(chain.latest_price)) + '<br>' + small(chain.baseline_date + ' → ' + chain.latest_date + ' · ' + chain.price_basis), esc(Math.abs(chain.net_reduction_rate) < .0001 ? '区间报价未变' : chain.net_reduction_rate > 0 ? '净降' + pct(chain.net_reduction_rate) : '净涨' + pct(-chain.net_reduction_rate)), esc('下调' + (finite(chain.downward_chain_rate) ? pct(chain.downward_chain_rate) : '未观察到')) + '<br>' + small('回升' + (finite(chain.upward_chain_rate) ? pct(chain.upward_chain_rate) : '未观察到')) ]));
    html += '<h4>每次通知与价格观察</h4><div class="pp-samples-wrap">' + table(['日期 / 时间', '平台 / 店铺 / 精确身份', '调前 → 调后', '本次幅度', '状态 / 依据'], events.map(event => {
      const when = event.record_type === 'observed_interval' ? event.interval_start + ' → ' + event.interval_end : date(event.notice_at || event.date);
      const identity = event.exact_listing ? scopeLabel(event) : [event.platform || (event.platform_scope || []).join(' / '), event.store || '店铺未列', event.shop_sku].filter(Boolean).join(' / ') || '平台 / 店铺未列';
      const precision = event.record_type === 'observed_interval' ? '报价观察区间，非生效日期' : event.date_precision || '日级';
      return [esc(when) + '<br>' + small(precision), esc(identity) + (event.listing_id ? '<br>' + small(event.listing_id) : '<br>' + small('不能据SKU级通知覆盖所有店铺')) + (event.warehouse ? '<br>' + small('仓范围：' + event.warehouse) : ''), priceCell(event), changeCell(event), esc(event.execution_status || event.notification_status || '') + '<br>' + small(event.summary || '') + sources(event)];
    })) + '</div></section>';
    return html;
  }

  root.ppPriceUi = { badges, summaryLine, detail };
})(window);

</script>
<script>
/* 主列只显示未含累计仓储利润；含仓储情景和逐单成本在证据及SKU详情中一致呈现。 */
(function (root) {
  'use strict';
  const model = root.ppProfitModel;
  if (!model?.ready || !root.ppPriceUi) throw Error('利润与调价数据未就绪');
  const finite = value => typeof value === 'number' && Number.isFinite(value);
  const esc = value => cxEsc(String(value ?? ''));
  const small = value => '<small class="pp-sub">' + esc(value) + '</small>';
  const pct = value => finite(value) ? (value * 100).toFixed(2) + '%' : '无同SKU样本';
  const money = value => finite(value) ? cxMoney(value) : '未取得';
  const platformLabel = value => value === 'Shopify' ? '独立站' : value;
  const scopeText = summary => summary ? summary.platformCount + '平台／' + summary.storeCount + '店铺 · '
    + summary.stores.map(value => value.replace('Shopify', '独立站')).join('、') : '无可用自身历史订单';
  const sampleText = summary => summary ? summary.periodStart + '—' + summary.periodEnd + ' · ' + summary.sampleCount + '单' : '未取得同SKU完整成本样本';
  function margin(value) { return finite(value) ? '<span class="pp-margin ' + (value < 0 ? 'loss' : '') + '">' + pct(value) + '</span>' : '<span class="pp-empty">无同SKU样本</span>'; }
  function storageText(summary) {
    if (!summary) return '无同SKU利润样本';
    if (summary.storageComplete) {
      return '运营 ' + money(summary.storageOperatingProfit) + '（' + pct(summary.storageOperatingMargin) + '）／真实 '
        + money(summary.storageTrueProfit) + '（' + pct(summary.storageTrueMargin) + '）';
    }
    const scope = summary.storageSampleCount + '/' + summary.sampleCount + '单仓费可算';
    const subset = summary.storageSubset;
    return '综合待核（' + scope + '）' + (subset ? '；可算仓费的订单小计，运营 '
      + pct(subset.operatingMargin) + '／真实 ' + pct(subset.trueMargin) : '');
  }
  function storageEvidence(row) {
    const warehouse = row.storageScenario || {}, reference = warehouse.reference;
    const details = (warehouse.allocations || []).map(item =>
      [item.warehouse, item.inbound, finite(item.days) ? item.days+'天' : '', item.carton || '', item.batch || ''].filter(Boolean).join(' · '));
    const source = [warehouse.source, warehouse.scopeLabel, ...details].filter(Boolean).join('；');
    const referenceText = reference ? '同仓批次参考 ' + money(reference.amount) + '／单 · ' + (reference.inboundDates || []).join('、')
      + ' · 仓龄' + (reference.minDays === reference.maxDays ? reference.minDays : reference.minDays+'–'+reference.maxDays)
      + '天 · 未配到本单批号' : '';
    const billed = finite(warehouse.observedFee) ? small('已见账单 '+money(warehouse.observedFee)+' · '
      +warehouse.billDays+'/'+warehouse.billDaysExpected+'天 · 缺日 '+warehouse.missingBillingDays.join('、')+'按原报价补估')
      : warehouse.billDays ? small('原账单覆盖 '+warehouse.billDays+'/'+warehouse.billDays+'个计费日（含订单日）') : '';
    const partial = finite(warehouse.partialAmount) ? small('已见账单小计 '+money(warehouse.partialAmount)+' · 待核缺日') : '';
    return small(source) + billed + partial + (referenceText ? small(referenceText) : '');
  }
  function explain(result) {
    const summary = result.selected;
    return !summary ? '未找到此SKU自身完整订单利润；按已确认规则不借其他SKU估算。'
      : (result.isForecast ? '通知价预估；同SKU历史成本重算，不是降价后实际结算利润。' : '同SKU最近有效订单综合；按收入加权，未含累计仓储。')
      + '样本 ' + sampleText(summary) + '；' + scopeText(summary) + '。';
  }
  const priorBuild = sqBuildRow;
  sqBuildRow = function (sku, filters) {
    const row = priorBuild(sku, filters), profit = model.forSku(sku, filters), summary = profit.selected;
    return { ...row, operatingMargin: summary?.operatingMargin ?? null, realMargin: summary?.trueMargin ?? null,
      storageOperatingMargin: summary?.storageOperatingMargin ?? null, storageRealMargin: summary?.storageTrueMargin ?? null,
      storageProfitComplete: summary?.storageComplete ?? false, profitSampleCount: summary?.sampleCount ?? 0,
      profitSampleStart: summary?.periodStart ?? null, profitSampleEnd: summary?.periodEnd ?? null,
      profitPlatformCount: summary?.platformCount ?? 0, profitStoreCount: summary?.storeCount ?? 0,
      profitStores: summary?.stores?.join('；') || '', profitDate: summary ? summary.periodStart + '—' + summary.periodEnd : '',
      profitBasis: profit.isForecast ? 'notice_forecast' : summary ? 'same_sku_recent20' : 'missing', isEstimate: Boolean(summary),
      profitStatus: profit.status, sourceNote: explain(profit), referenceKey: 'same-sku-20|' + sku + '|' + (filters.platform || '') + '|' + (filters.store || ''),
      pureProfitExcludesStorage: true, noCrossSkuFallback: true, _profit20: profit };
  };
  const priorCell = sqUiCell;
  sqUiCell = function (column, row) {
    const profit = row._profit20 || model.forSku(row.sku, sqUiState.filters), summary = profit.selected;
    if (column === 'identity') {
      const html = priorCell(column, row), badges = root.ppPriceUi.badges(row.sku, sqUiState.filters);
      return html.replace('</button>', '</button>' + badges) + root.ppPriceUi.summaryLine(row.sku, sqUiState.filters);
    }
    if (column === 'operatingMargin' || column === 'realMargin') return margin(column === 'operatingMargin' ? row.operatingMargin : row.realMargin)
      + small('未含累计仓储') + (summary ? small(profit.isForecast ? '通知价预估' : '最近' + summary.sampleCount + '单综合') : small('不使用其他SKU样本'));
    if (column === 'scope') {
      const warehouses = [...new Set(profit.samples.flatMap(sample => sample.storageScenario?.allocations?.map(row=>row.warehouse)
        || [sample.storageScenario?.warehouse || (sample.warehouseStatus==='同SKU+订单出库/账单证明' ? sample.warehouse : '')]).filter(Boolean))];
      return priorCell(column, row).replace('利润参考仓：','报价参考仓：')
        + (warehouses.length ? small('样本订单出库仓 '+warehouses.join('、')) : '')
        + (summary ? small('利润综合 '+summary.platformCount+'平台／'+summary.storeCount+'店铺') : '');
    }
    if (column === 'price' && profit.isForecast) {
      const prices = profit.samples.map(sample => sample.price), low = Math.min(...prices), high = Math.max(...prices);
      return '<span class="sq-cell-number">' + money(low) + (low !== high ? '～' + money(high) : '') + '</span>' + small('通知含运价 · 预估场景')
        + small('原参考报价 ' + money(row.displayPrice) + ' · ' + (row.priceDate || '日期未核')) + small(profit.samples[0].priceScenario.notice_at);
    }
    if (column === 'evidence') return '<div class="pp-evidence"><span class="pp-status ' + (profit.isForecast ? 'estimate' : !summary ? 'missing' : '') + '">'
      + esc(profit.status) + '</span>' + small(sampleText(summary)) + (summary ? small(scopeText(summary)) : small('仅限此SKU自身历史'))
      + '<div class="pp-storage"><strong>毛利（含仓储费）</strong><br>' + esc(storageText(summary)) + '</div>'
      + small('已核仓储证据快照 ' + model.storageDate)
      + '<button type="button" class="pp-detail-button" data-pp-open-profit="' + esc(row.sku) + '">逐单利润、毛利与调价依据</button></div>';
    return priorCell(column, row);
  };
  function metric(label, value, note, storage = false) {
    return '<div class="pp-metric ' + (storage ? 'storage' : '') + '"><span>' + esc(label) + '</span><strong>'
      + (finite(value) ? pct(value) : '—') + '</strong><small>' + esc(note) + '</small></div>';
  }
  function summaryHtml(profit) {
    const s = profit.selected;
    return '<section class="pp-section"><h3>运营与真实利润率 ' + root.ppPriceUi.badges(profit.sku, currentFilters()) + '</h3>'
      + '<p>' + esc(explain(profit)) + '</p><div class="pp-metrics">'
      + metric('运营利润率', s?.operatingMargin, '未含仓储及模型预留') + metric('真实利润率', s?.trueMargin, '未含仓储及模型预留')
      + metric('运营毛利率', s?.storageOperatingMargin, s?.storageComplete ? money(s.storageOperatingProfit)+' · '+(s.storageEstimateCount?'含订单日测算':s.storageAllocatedCount?'按原账单分摊':'订单仓租实据') : '部分批次待核', true)
      + metric('真实毛利率', s?.storageTrueMargin, s?.storageComplete ? money(s.storageTrueProfit)+' · '+(s.storageEstimateCount?'含订单日测算':s.storageAllocatedCount?'按原账单分摊':'订单仓租实据') : '部分批次待核', true) + '</div>'
      + (s ? '<p>' + esc(scopeText(s)) + (s.storeCount === 1 ? '；仅此平台／店铺有可用样本。' : '。') + '</p>' : '')
      + '<div class="pp-note">单笔利润按运营采购或真实采购分别核算。毛利＝单笔利润－该订单仓储费。仓库出入库作业费单列并保留。模型售后预留仅供参考，实际退款随订单凭证复核。</div>'
      + '<p>先用订单实际出库与调拨批次。按费率测算时采用订单日库龄和分包体积；本区足量优先，本区缺货才从其他区域可用老库存分配。调拨后的计费起点沿账单和批次记录核对。批号或历史可用不足时单列同仓参考。</p>'
      + (s && !s.storageComplete ? '<div class="pp-note pp-warning">' + esc(storageText(s)) + '。缺证仓费保留待核。</div>' : '')
      + (profit.isForecast ? '<div class="pp-note pp-warning">采用群通知价情景。店铺／Listing为可比报价的参考身份，准确逐店生效时间未核；通知价不套用Wayfair供货价。平台费和广告沿用各自同单费率，其余费用保留；税或安装成本无法对应的样本不参与改价估算。</div>' : '')
      + '</section>';
  }
  function sourceText(source) { return [source.file || source.path || '', source.sheet || '', source.row != null ? '第' + source.row + '行' : ''].filter(Boolean).join(' · '); }
  function samplesHtml(samples, title, isForecast) {
    if (!samples.length) return '<section class="pp-section"><h3>' + esc(title) + '</h3><p>无同SKU自身完整利润样本。不会把其他SKU或近似商品码的成本当成这款的订单。</p></section>';
    return '<section class="pp-section"><h3>' + esc(title) + '</h3><p>按订单日期从近到远，最多20笔有效订单；以下每单利润按订单全部源数量计算，原费用与来源可展开查看。</p>'
      + '<div class="pp-samples-wrap">' + cxTable(['订单日期／平台店铺', '订单／SKU', '数量', isForecast ? '通知价／收入USD' : '历史含运收入USD', '利润 · 运营／真实', '仓储费／单及库龄依据', '毛利（含仓储）· 运营／真实', '费用及来源'], samples.map(row => [
        esc(row.sampleDate) + small(platformLabel(row.platform) + ' / ' + row.store), esc(row.orderId) + small(row.sku), cxQty(row.quantity),
        (isForecast ? money(row.price) + small('原每套 ' + money(row.referencePrice)) : '') + small('订单收入 ' + money(row.revenue)),
        margin(row.operatingMargin) + ' / ' + margin(row.trueMargin) + small('订单利润 ' + money(row.pureOperatingProfitTotal) + ' / ' + money(row.pureTrueProfitTotal)),
        money(row.warehouseFeeTotal) + storageEvidence(row),
        (finite(row.storageOperatingMargin) ? pct(row.storageOperatingMargin) + ' / ' + pct(row.storageTrueMargin) + small('毛利 ' + money(row.storageOperatingProfitTotal) + ' / ' + money(row.storageTrueProfitTotal)) : '仓储费待核'),
        '<details><summary>成本／凭证</summary><p>运营采购 ' + money(row.operatingPurchase * row.quantity) + '；真实采购 ' + money(row.truePurchase * row.quantity) + '</p>'
          + '<p>' + Object.entries(row.componentTotals).filter(([key]) => key !== 'storage').map(([key, value]) => esc(({head:'头程',tariff:'关税',handling:'作业',platformFee:'平台费',advertising:'广告',tail:'尾程',consumptionTax:'消费税',installation:'安装',aftersalesReserve:'售后储备'})[key] || key) + ' ' + money(value)).join('；') + '</p>'
          + (finite(row.modelAftersalesReserve) ? '<p>原模型售后预留 '+money(row.modelAftersalesReserve)+' · 本次未扣</p>' : '')
          + (row.storageScenario?.costSourceNote ? '<p>'+esc(row.storageScenario.costSourceNote)+'</p>' : '')
          + '<p class="pp-source">' + (row.sourceLineage || [row.source || {}]).map(source => esc(sourceText(source))).join('<br>') + '</p>'
          + (row.priceScenario ? '<p>通知 ' + esc(row.priceScenario.notice_at || row.priceScenario.date) + '；逐店生效未核；报价Listing ' + esc(row.priceScenario.listing_id || '') + '</p>' : '') + '</details>'
      ])) + '</div></section>';
  }
  function currentFilters() {
    if (cxState.view === 'query') return sqUiState.filters;
    return { platform: cxState.sales?.platform || '', store: sxState?.store || '', evidenceMode: 'current' };
  }
  function profitDetail(sku) {
    const filters = currentFilters(), profit = model.forSku(sku, filters);
    const rejected = profit.rejected.slice(0, 30);
    return summaryHtml(profit) + samplesHtml(profit.samples, profit.isForecast ? '通知价下每单预估利润' : '最近有效订单 · 逐单利润', profit.isForecast)
      + (profit.isForecast ? '<details class="pp-section"><summary>原成交价下最近有效订单综合 · 运营 ' + pct(profit.historical?.operatingMargin) + '／真实 ' + pct(profit.historical?.trueMargin) + '</summary>'
        + samplesHtml(profit.historicalSamples, '历史成交价与原成本', false) + '</details>' : '')
      + root.ppPriceUi.detail(sku, filters)
      + '<details class="pp-section"><summary>取样范围与未入选原因</summary><p>可用同SKU订单 ' + profit.validSampleCount + '笔；本次取最近最多20笔。渠道按当前平台／店铺条件；销量日期区间不截断自身历史成本样本，样本日期另列。</p>'
      + '<p>只有订单收入、采购、头程／关税／作业／尾程及对应费用依据完整，且有效的正式销售订单才参与。取消、测试、纯补发、已退货及未核费用不作为零成本样本。</p>'
      + (rejected.length ? cxTable(['订单日期', '平台／店铺', '订单', '未入选原因'], rejected.map(row => [esc(row.sampleDate), esc(row.platform + ' / ' + row.store), esc(row.orderId), esc((row.missingFields || []).join('；'))])) : '<p>当前可见渠道未另见被拒绝的成本样本。</p>') + '</details>';
  }
  let activeSku = '';
  const priorDetail = cxRenderSku;
  cxRenderSku = function () {
    priorDetail();
    const sku = cxState.detail?.sku || (cxState.view === 'query' ? sqDetailRow?.sku : '');
    if (!sku) return;
    const tabs = cxEl('cxDetailTabs');
    const title = cxEl('cxDetailTitle'); if (title && !title.querySelector('.pp-badges')) title.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend', root.ppPriceUi.badges(sku, currentFilters()));
    if (tabs && !tabs.querySelector('[data-pp-tab]')) tabs.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend', '<button type="button" data-pp-tab="' + esc(sku) + '" class="' + (activeSku === sku ? 'active' : '') + '">利润与调价</button>');
    if (activeSku === sku || cxState.view === 'query' && sqDetailTab === 'finance') {
      if (activeSku === sku) tabs?.querySelectorAll('button').forEach(button => button.classList.toggle('active', button.hasAttribute('data-pp-tab')));
      cxEl('cxDetailBody').innerHTML = profitDetail(sku);
    } else if (cxState.view === 'query' && sqDetailTab === 'overview' || cxState.view !== 'query' && cxState.detail?.tab === 'overview') {
      cxEl('cxDetailBody').insertAdjacentHTML('afterbegin', summaryHtml(model.forSku(sku, currentFilters())));
    }
  };
  root.addEventListener('click', event => {
    const button = event.target.closest('[data-pp-open-profit],[data-pp-tab]');
    if (button) {
      event.preventDefault(); event.stopImmediatePropagation();
      const sku = button.dataset.ppOpenProfit || button.dataset.ppTab;
      activeSku = sku;
      if (button.hasAttribute('data-pp-open-profit')) sqOpenSku(sku); else cxRenderSku();
    } else if (event.target.closest('[data-cx-detail-tab],[data-sq-detail-tab],[data-sq-sku],[data-cx-sku]')) activeSku = '';
  }, true);
  const priorCoverage = sqUiCoverage;
  sqUiCoverage = function (result) { return priorCoverage(result)
    .replace('默认按当前价格查询；历史补充需单独选择，并逐行标记样本日期。售价、利润保持同平台／店铺／仓库的引用作用域。', '利润采用当前已标注的通知价场景或同SKU最近20笔有效订单综合，按收入加权；按平台／店铺先筛选后取样。原成交与通知价情景分别展示。')
    .replace(/每SKU固定选择一个同作用域利润参考/g, '每SKU采用最近最多20笔自身有效订单综合')
    + '<details class="pp-contract"><summary>利润与调价计算口径</summary><p>运营／真实利润率均未含累计仓储与模型售后预留。最近最多20笔同SKU完整有效订单按收入加权综合；不足20笔用现有样本。'
    + '“通知价预估＋最近20单”采用有明确可比平台店铺的通知价情景；“只看最近20单”只看原成交价。没有自身样本时不跨SKU兜底。</p><p>运营／真实含仓储毛利率在证据与日期及SKU详情中另列；未知仓储不按0计算。调价通知时间与后台实际生效时间分别保留。价格累计只计算同平台／店铺／Listing且口径可比的基线，不能直接相加百分比。</p></details>'; };
  const priorRender = sqRenderQuery;
  sqRenderQuery = function () {
    const result = priorRender(), host = cxEl('sqQueryBody');
    if (!host) return result;
    const evidence = host.querySelector('[data-sq-field="evidenceMode"]');
    if (evidence) for (const option of Array.from(evidence.options)) {
      if (option.value === 'withStorage') { option.remove(); continue; }
      option.textContent = option.value === 'current' ? '通知价预估＋最近20单' : option.value === 'withHistory' ? '只看最近20单（原成交价）' : '无同SKU利润样本';
    }
    const missing = host.querySelector('[data-sq-preset="missing"]'); if (missing) missing.textContent = '无自身利润样本SKU';
    for (const tag of host.querySelectorAll('.sq-filter-chip')) if (/现价优先＋历史补充|利润待补/.test(tag.textContent)) {
      const button = tag.querySelector('button'), mode = sqUiState.filters.evidenceMode;
      const label = mode === 'withHistory' ? '只看最近20单（原成交价）' : mode === 'missing' ? '无同SKU利润样本' : '通知价预估＋最近20单';
      tag.replaceChildren(document.createTextNode(label), ...(button ? [button] : []));
    }
    const note = host.querySelector('.sq-result-note'); if (note) note.textContent += ' 利润主列未含累计仓储；同SKU最近最多20笔有效成本样本独立标注日期，支持平台／店铺筛选。';
    return result;
  };
  const priorExport = sqUiExport;
  sqUiExport = function () {
    const rows = sqExportRows({ ...sqUiState.filters }, { ...sqUiState.sort }), bySku = new Map(rows.map(row => [row.sku, row]));
    const download = cxDownloadCsv;
    const fields = [['operatingMargin','未含累计仓储运营利润率'],['realMargin','未含累计仓储真实利润率'],['storageOperatingMargin','含订单仓储运营毛利率'],['storageRealMargin','含订单仓储真实毛利率'],
      ['profitSampleCount','自身有效订单样本数'],['profitSampleStart','利润样本开始'],['profitSampleEnd','利润样本结束'],['profitPlatformCount','利润平台数'],['profitStoreCount','利润店铺数'],['profitStores','利润平台店铺'],['profitStatus','利润计算状态']];
    cxDownloadCsv = function (name, headers, values) {
      if (!String(name).startsWith('SKU数据查询_')) return download(name, headers, values);
      const skuIndex = headers.indexOf('商品SKU'); if (skuIndex < 0) throw Error('利润导出缺少商品SKU定位列');
      const extra = ['仓储证据快照日','禁止跨SKU均值兜底','可核仓储订单数','已知仓储费USD','运营毛利USD','真实毛利USD'];
      return download(name.replace(/2026111[36]/, '20261117'), [...headers, ...fields.map(field => field[1]), ...extra], values.map(value => {
        const row = bySku.get(value[skuIndex]); if (!row) throw Error('利润导出与当前查询结果不一致');
        return [...value, ...fields.map(([key]) => key.includes('Margin') ? finite(row[key]) ? pct(row[key]) : null : row[key] ?? null), model.storageDate, true,
          row._profit20?.selected?.storageSampleCount ?? 0, row._profit20?.selected?.storageTotal ?? null,
          row._profit20?.selected?.storageOperatingProfit ?? null, row._profit20?.selected?.storageTrueProfit ?? null];
      }));
    };
    try { return priorExport(); } finally { cxDownloadCsv = download; }
  };
  const priorView = setPageView;
  setPageView = function (view) {
    const result = priorView.apply(this, arguments);
    if (view === 'query') cxEl('subtitle').textContent = '净销量已核至' + root.salesRefreshModel.coverage.native.end + ' · 最近20笔同SKU有效订单利润 · 含订单仓储费毛利单列';
    return result;
  };
  // 旧默认“现价/历史补充”现在改为已明示的通知价情景＋最近20单；不重置销量日期或其他条件。
  sqUiState.filters.evidenceMode = 'current'; sqUiState.draft.evidenceMode = 'current';
  model.clearCaches(); root.spQueryPeriodModel?.clearCaches(); sqSalesCache.clear();
  if (cxState.view === 'query') sqRenderQuery();
  root.ppProfitUi = { ready: true, profitDetail, summaryHtml };
  const marker = document.createElement('span'); marker.id = 'profitPriceReady'; marker.hidden = true; document.body.appendChild(marker);
})(window);

</script>
